│ **Deviation:** Clear human_decision after routing instead of in              │
│ process_decision_node                                                        │
│ **Document:** docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md §8.4                      │
│ **Reason:** Doc 3 §8.4 clears human_decision in process_decision_node, but   │
│ §8.2 route_after_decision reads it AFTER that node runs. Clearing before     │
│ routing causes graph to default to "end" instead of correct branch.          │
│ Resolution: clear in advance_node (approve path), structure_node (reject     │
│ path), and validate_node (modify path).                                      │
│ **Approved by:** Co-Developer review + plan approval                         │
│ **Incorporated into spec:** Updated docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md     │
│ §8.4 process_decision_node and advance_node.                                 │

│                                                                              │
│ # PRINCIPLES                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ 1. **Methodology before execution** - Know HOW before doing WHAT             │
│ 2. **Iteration improves quality** - V1→V2→V3 catches gaps                    │
│ 3. **Human judgment at gates** - Agent recommends, human decides             │
│ 4. **Traceability everywhere** - Every output traces to its source           │
│ 5. **Explicit contracts** - Inputs and outputs formally specified            │
│ 6. **Validation at every phase** - Don't proceed with invalid state          │
│ 7. **Instance inheritance** - Build on prior instances, don't repeat         │
│ 8. **Stated vs. Actual** - Preserve what was asked; derive what needs        │
│ solving                                                                      │
│ 9. **Completeness in V3** - No ellipses, no placeholders, no handwaving      │
│                                                                              │
│ ---                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ # GLOSSARY                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ | Term | Definition |                                                        │
│ |------|------------|                                                        │
│ | **Instance 0** | Abstract methodology; general and abstract; defines HOW   │
│ to reason |                                                                  │
│ | **Instance 1** | Software implementation; general and specific; implements │
│ the methodology |                                                            │
│ | **Instance N** | Domain application; specific and situated; solves domain  │
│ problems |                                                                   │
│ | **Pass 1** | Methodology creation pass; produces V3 documents; autonomous  │
│ |                                                                            │
│ | **Pass 2** | Methodology execution pass; produces deliverables; gated |    │
│ | **V1/V2/V3** | Iteration versions; each refines the previous; V3 must be   │
│ complete |                                                                   │
│ | **Data Sheet** | Document defining input/output contracts, schemas,        │
│ validation rules |                                                           │
│ | **To Do List** | Document defining executable tasks with validation hooks  │
│ |                                                                            │
│ | **Guidance** | Document providing context, principles, and anti-patterns | │
│ | **Detailed Procedure** | Document providing algorithmic instructions and   │
│ state machine |                                                              │
│ | **Stated Problem** | What the user literally asked |                       │
│ | **Core Problem** | What actually needs to be solved |                      │
│ | **Hard Constraint** | Non-negotiable limit; violation = failure |          │
│ | **Soft Constraint** | Preference with trade-offs |                         │
│ | **FR** | Functional Requirement (FR-NNN); what solution does |             │
│ | **NFR** | Non-Functional Requirement (NFR-NNN); how well solution performs │
│ |                                                                            │
│ | **CR** | Constraint Requirement (CR-NNN); limits solution must respect |   │
│ | **CAP** | Capability Objective (CAP-NNN); from functional requirements |   │
│ | **QUAL** | Quality Objective (QUAL-NNN); from non-functional requirements  │
│ |                                                                            │
│ | **COMP** | Compliance Objective (COMP-NNN); from constraint requirements | │
│ | **Primary Objective** | From must requirements; minimum viable |           │
│ | **Secondary Objective** | From should requirements; target state |         │
│ | **Tertiary Objective** | From could requirements; aspirational |           │
│ | **Elicitation** | Process of asking clarifying questions per Clarification │
│ Policy |                                                                     │
│                                                                              │

│                                                                              │
│ *This prompt defines the process. Apply it to your problem domain to produce │
│ rigorous, traceable solutions with human oversight.*                         │

     to the same Persistence Layer. It must import the Settings to get the DB
     connection string but should run independently.

  Plan (P6.1)

  1. Create File: `tools/verify_methodology.py`
   * Imports: asyncio, argparse, sqlalchemy (async engine), sys/os (to path-hack
     apps/api for config).
   * Arguments:
       * --workflow-id: (Optional) UUID of workflow to check. If omitted, fetch
         the most recent workflow.
       * --steps: List of step numbers to check (default: 1 2 3).
   * Logic:
       1. Connect to PostgreSQL using apps.api.config.settings.postgres_url.
       2. Query the artifacts table filtering by:
           * workflow_id
           * pass_type = 'definition'
           * artifact_type = 'methodology_doc'
           * step_number in provided list.
       3. Group/Count results by step_number, document_type, document_version.
       4. Validation:
           * Expect 12 documents per step (4 types × 3 versions).
           * Total expected: 36 (for steps 1-3).
       5. Output:
           * Print a matrix of found vs. expected documents.
           * Exit code 0 if all present, 1 if missing.

   * We will rely on apps.api.config for the connection string.

✦ Evaluation of Senior Developer's Plan:

  1. Critique
   * Strengths: The plan correctly identifies the Gate A criteria (36 documents)
     and the necessary output format/exit codes. It appropriately reuses the
     project's configuration and models.
   * Weakness (Architecture): The plan proposes modifying the production
     codebase (apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py) to add a
     method (count_methodology_docs_grouped) solely for the use of an external
     verification CLI tool.
   * Correction: This violates the principle of separation of concerns.
     Verification tools should observe the system state using existing
     models/interfaces without forcing changes to the application's internal
     repository logic unless that logic is needed by the API itself.

  2. Directives
   * Do not modify apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py.
   * Implement the query logic directly within tools/verify_methodology.py. Use
     sqlalchemy.select with func.count against the Artifact model. This keeps
     the verification logic isolated in tools/.
   * Ensure tools/verify_methodology.py handles the sys.path correctly to import
     apps.api.config and apps.api.infrastructure.db.models.

  3. Decision
  Proceed with the creation of tools/verify_methodology.py ONLY.

    1. --workflow-id UUID vs String — Agree, must fix

  workflows.workflow_id (public ID), resolve to UUID | Co-Developer-1 is correct
  |

    The public API uses string workflow IDs like "wf-abc123". Users will copy
  these from API responses. My plan asks for the internal UUID which users won't
  have. Fix: accept string, resolve via WorkflowRepository.

    2. "All Workflows" Default — Agree, must fix

    | My Plan                           | Co-Developer-1
  | Verdict                   |
    
  |-----------------------------------|-----------------------------------------
  ----------|---------------------------|
    | Default to scanning all workflows | Gate A is per-workflow (36 docs for
  ONE workflow) | Co-Developer-1 is correct |

    Aggregating across workflows is misleading. A workflow with 18 docs +
  another with 18 docs = 36 total but neither passes Gate A. Fix: require
  --workflow-id OR auto-select most recent workflow (with warning).

    3. Adding Method to ArtifactRepository — Agree, prefer self-contained

    | My Plan                                                 | Co-Developer-1
  | Verdict                                |

  list_for_workflow() + in-script grouping | Co-Developer-1 has the better
  approach |

    Rationale:
    - P6 is verification phase, not feature development
    - Adding methods to production code increases surface area and test burden
    - A verification tool should be self-contained and use existing APIs
    - Python grouping of ~36 artifacts is trivial performance-wise

    4. Content Validation Beyond Counting — Agree, must add

    | My Plan         | Co-Developer-1                         | Verdict
  |
    
  |-----------------|----------------------------------------|------------------
  ---------|
    | Count rows only | Validate content_markdown is non-empty | Co-Developer-1
  is correct |

    Gate A says "methodology exists" — an empty doc doesn't satisfy this. Must
  also enforce:
    - artifact_type = 'methodology_doc'
    - pass_type = 'definition'
    - content_markdown IS NOT NULL AND content_markdown != ''

    CHANGES FROM ORIGINAL PLAN:

    1. NO modifications to apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py
       - Use ArtifactRepository.list_for_workflow() and filter/group in Python

  ⏺ Evaluation of Co-Developer-1 Feedback (Round 2)

    This feedback refines and confirms the previous review. Let me assess each
  element:

    Confirmed Decisions

    | Item                       | Status    |
    |----------------------------|-----------|
    | 36 docs target (3×4×3)     | ✅ Agreed |
    | CLI tool in tools/         | ✅ Agreed |

    Key Refinement: --workflow-id Required

    | Previous Revision                | Co-Developer-1 (Round 2) | My
  Assessment       |
    
  |----------------------------------|--------------------------|---------------
  ------|
    | Optional, with --latest fallback | Required (string)        | Agree with
  required |

    Rationale for requiring --workflow-id:
    - Explicit > implicit — user knows exactly what they're verifying
    - Avoids ambiguity about "latest" semantics (by created_at? updated_at?)
    - Simpler implementation, fewer edge cases
    - If --latest is needed, it can be a future enhancement

    Content Validation Emphasis

    Co-Developer-1 is explicit: "Empty docs must fail."

    This means the tool must check:
    # Not just existence, but valid content
    if artifact.content_markdown is None or artifact.content_markdown.strip() ==
  "":
        status = "EMPTY"  # Fails Gate A

    Uniqueness Constraint

    New detail: "enforce exactly one per combination"

    This catches a subtle bug — if somehow two v1 data_sheets exist for Step 1,
  that's anomalous. The tool should:
    - Expect exactly 1 artifact per (step, doc_type, version) tuple
    - Flag duplicates as warnings or errors

    ---
    Final Perspective

    I fully agree with Co-Developer-1's recommended shape. It is:

    1. Minimal — single file, no production code changes
    2. Explicit — requires workflow-id, no magic defaults
    3. Thorough — validates content, not just counts
    4. Clear — defined exit codes and output format

    Implementation Summary

    tools/verify_methodology.py

    INPUTS:
      --workflow-id   STRING (required) - public workflow ID
      --steps         INT... (optional) - default: 1 2 3
      --database-url  STRING (optional) - override from settings

    BEHAVIOR:
      1. Resolve workflow_id string → internal UUID
      2. Query artifacts WHERE:
         - workflow_id = <resolved UUID>
         - artifact_type = 'methodology_doc'
         - pass_type = 'definition'
         - step_number IN (1, 2, 3)
      3. Group by (step_number, document_type, document_version)
      4. For each of 36 expected combinations:
         - MISSING: no artifact found
         - EMPTY: artifact exists but content_markdown is null/blank
         - OK: artifact exists with content
         - DUPLICATE: more than one artifact (warning)
      5. Exit 0 if all 36 are OK, else exit 1

    EXIT CODES:
      0 - PASS (36 valid docs)
      1 - FAIL (missing/empty docs)
      2 - ERROR (database connection)
      3 - ERROR (workflow not found)

    OUTPUT:
      Grid showing status per step/type/version + summary

   P6.1 Plan: Gate A Verification Tool (Final)

   Objective

   Create tools/verify_methodology.py - CLI tool to verify 36 methodology docs
  exist with valid content (Gate
    A criterion).

   Gate A Criterion

   - 3 steps × 4 document types × 3 versions = 36 methodology documents
   - Document types: data_sheet, todo_list, guidance, detailed_procedure
   - Document versions: v1, v2, v3
   - Steps (MVP): 1, 2, 3
   - Content must be non-empty (not just existence)

   Files to Create

   | File                        | Action | Purpose                 |
   |-----------------------------|--------|-------------------------|
   | tools/verify_methodology.py | Create | Self-contained CLI tool |

   No modifications to production code in apps/api/.

   CLI Specification

   Arguments

   | Argument       | Type   | Required | Default       | Description
  |
   
  |----------------|--------|----------|---------------|------------------------
  --------------|
   | --workflow-id  | STRING | Yes      | —             | Public workflow ID
  (e.g., wf-abc123) |
   | --steps        | INT... | No       | 1 2 3         | Steps to verify
  |
   | --database-url | STRING | No       | From settings | Override DB connection
  |
   | --verbose / -v | Flag   | No       | False         | Show detailed output
  |

   Exit Codes

   | Code | Meaning                                     |
   |------|---------------------------------------------|
   | 0    | PASS - All 36 docs found with valid content |
   | 1    | FAIL - Missing or empty docs                |
   | 2    | ERROR - Database connection failed          |
   | 3    | ERROR - Workflow not found                  |

   Behavior

   1. Resolve workflow ID: Accept public workflow_id string → resolve to
  internal UUID via WorkflowRepository
   2. Query artifacts with filters:
     - workflow_id = <resolved UUID>
     - artifact_type = 'methodology_doc'
     - pass_type = 'definition'
     - step_number IN (1, 2, 3)
   3. Group by (step_number, document_type, document_version)
   4. Validate each of 36 combinations:
     - MISSING - No artifact found
     - EMPTY - Artifact exists but content_markdown is null/blank
     - OK - Artifact exists with valid content
     - DUPLICATE - More than one artifact (warning)
   5. Exit 0 only if all 36 are OK

   Output Format

   Gate A Verification Report
   ==========================
   Workflow: wf-abc123
   Steps: 1, 2, 3

   Step 1: problem_definition
   --------------------------
   Document Type        v1       v2       v3
   data_sheet           OK       OK       OK
   todo_list            OK       OK       OK
   guidance             OK       MISSING  OK
   detailed_procedure   OK       OK       EMPTY

   Step 2: requirements
   --------------------------
   ...

   Summary
   =======
   Expected: 36
   OK:       34
   Missing:  1
   Empty:    1
   Status:   FAIL

   Implementation Approach

   Self-Contained Design

   - Use existing ArtifactRepository.list_for_workflow()
   - Filter and group in Python (no new repository methods)
   - Import via sys.path pointing to apps/api

   Key Query Logic

   # Fetch all artifacts for workflow
   artifacts = await artifact_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_uuid)

   # Filter to methodology docs
   methodology_docs = [
       a for a in artifacts
       if a.artifact_type == "methodology_doc"
       and a.pass_type == PassType.DEFINITION
       and a.step_number in steps_to_check
   ]

   # Group by (step, type, version) and validate content
   for step in steps:
       for doc_type in DocumentType:
           for version in DocumentVersion:
               matches = [a for a in methodology_docs if ...]
               if len(matches) == 0:
                   status = "MISSING"
               elif not matches[0].content_markdown or not
  matches[0].content_markdown.strip():
                   status = "EMPTY"
               else:
                   status = "OK"

   Verification Command

   python tools/verify_methodology.py --workflow-id wf-abc123
   # Exit 0 if all 36 docs exist with content

   Dependencies

   - apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py (existing, read-only)
   - apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/workflow.py (existing, read-only)
   - apps/api/infrastructure/db/models/enums.py (DocumentType, DocumentVersion,
  PassType)
   - apps/api/config.py (settings.postgres_url)

  </information only>
  No action required.

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview

   2. Constraint: Do not modify production code in apps/api.
   3. Input: Require --workflow-id (string) to target a specific workflow; map

   4. Validation: Check not just existence but also that content_markdown is

      3=No Workflow).

│ 103                                                                          │

│ 105     result = VerificationResult()                                        │
│ 106                                                                          │
│ 107     # Initialize matrix                                                  │
│ 108     for step in steps:                                                   │
│ 109         result.matrix[step] = {}                                         │
│ 110         for doc_type in REQUIRED_DOC_TYPES:                              │
│ 111             result.matrix[step][doc_type] = {}                           │
│ 112             for version in REQUIRED_VERSIONS:                            │
│ 113                 result.matrix[step][doc_type][version] = "MISSING"       │
│ 114                 result.total_expected += 1                               │
│ 115                                                                          │
│ 116     # Populate matrix                                                    │
│ 117     for step in steps:                                                   │
│ 118         for doc_type in REQUIRED_DOC_TYPES:                              │
│ 119             for version in REQUIRED_VERSIONS:                            │
│ 120                 # Find matching artifacts                                │
│ 121                 matches = [                                              │
│ 122                     a for a in artifacts                                 │
│ 123                     if a.step_number == step                             │
│ 124                     and a.document_type == doc_type                      │
│ 125                     and a.document_version == version                    │
│ 126                 ]                                                        │
│ 127                                                                          │
│ 128                 if not matches:                                          │
│ 129                     result.matrix[step][doc_type][version] = "MISSING"   │
│ 130                     result.missing_count += 1                            │
│ 131                 else:                                                    │
│ 132                     artifact = matches[0]                                │
│ 133                     content = artifact.content_markdown                  │
│ 134                                                                          │
│ 135                     if not content or not content.strip():               │
│ 136                         result.matrix[step][doc_type][version] = "EMPTY" │
│ 137                         result.empty_count += 1                          │
│ 138                     else:                                                │
│ 139                         result.matrix[step][doc_type][version] = "OK"    │
│ 140                         result.ok_count += 1                             │
│ 141                                                                          │
│ 142                     if len(matches) > 1:                                 │
│ 143                         # Warning only, doesn't fail the check if one is │
│     valid                                                                    │
│ 144                         result.duplicates_count += 1                     │
│ 145                                                                          │
│ 146     # 4. Print Report                                                    │
│ 147     print_report(workflow_id_str, steps, result)                         │
│ 148                                                                          │
│ 149     # 5. Determine Exit Code                                             │
│ 150     if result.ok_count == result.total_expected:                         │
│ 151         return 0                                                         │
│ 152     else:                                                                │
│ 153         return 1                                                         │

│ 165         # Header                                                         │

│     10}")                                                                    │
│ 167                                                                          │
│ 168         for doc_type in REQUIRED_DOC_TYPES:                              │

│ 179     print(f"OK:       {result.ok_count}")                                │
│ 180     print(f"Missing:  {result.missing_count}")                           │
│ 181     print(f"Empty:    {result.empty_count}")                             │
│ 182                                                                          │
│ 183     if result.duplicates_count > 0:                                      │
│ 184         print(f"Duplicates: {result.duplicates_count} (Warning)")        │
│ 185                                                                          │
│ 186     print(f"Status:   {'PASS' if result.ok_count ==                      │
│     result.total_expected else 'FAIL'}")                                     │
│ 187                                                                          │
│ 188 def main():                                                              │

       Objective

       Create tools/verify_methodology.py - CLI tool to verify 36 methodology
  docs exist with valid 
       content (Gate A criterion).

       No modifications to production code in apps/api/.

       Arguments

       | Argument       | Type   | Required | Default       | Description
  |
       
  |----------------|--------|----------|---------------|------------------------
  --------------|

  (e.g., wf-abc123) |

  |
       | --database-url | STRING | No       | From settings | Override DB
  connection               |
       | --verbose / -v | Flag   | No       | False         | Show detailed
  output                 |

       Exit Codes

       | Code | Meaning                                     |
       |------|---------------------------------------------|
       | 0    | PASS - All 36 docs found with valid content |
       | 1    | FAIL - Missing or empty docs                |
       | 2    | ERROR - Database connection failed          |
       | 3    | ERROR - Workflow not found                  |

       Behavior

       1. Resolve workflow ID: Accept public workflow_id string → resolve to
  internal UUID via 
       WorkflowRepository
       2. Query artifacts with filters:
         - workflow_id = <resolved UUID>
         - artifact_type = 'methodology_doc'
         - pass_type = 'definition'
         - step_number IN (1, 2, 3)
       3. Group by (step_number, document_type, document_version)
       4. Validate each of 36 combinations:
         - MISSING - No artifact found
         - EMPTY - Artifact exists but content_markdown is null/blank
         - OK - Artifact exists with valid content
         - DUPLICATE - More than one artifact (warning)
       5. Exit 0 only if all 36 are OK

       Output Format

       Gate A Verification Report
       ==========================
       Workflow: wf-abc123
       Steps: 1, 2, 3

       Step 1: problem_definition
       --------------------------

       data_sheet           OK       OK       OK
       todo_list            OK       OK       OK
       guidance             OK       MISSING  OK
       detailed_procedure   OK       OK       EMPTY

       Step 2: requirements
       --------------------------
       ...

       Summary
       =======
       Expected: 36
       OK:       34
       Missing:  1
       Empty:    1
       Status:   FAIL

       Implementation Approach

       Self-Contained Design

       - Use existing ArtifactRepository.list_for_workflow()
       - Filter and group in Python (no new repository methods)
       - Import via sys.path pointing to apps/api

       Key Query Logic

       # Fetch all artifacts for workflow
       artifacts = await artifact_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_uuid)

       # Filter to methodology docs
       methodology_docs = [
           a for a in artifacts
           if a.artifact_type == "methodology_doc"
           and a.pass_type == PassType.DEFINITION
           and a.step_number in steps_to_check
       ]

       # Group by (step, type, version) and validate content
       for step in steps:
           for doc_type in DocumentType:
               for version in DocumentVersion:
                   matches = [a for a in methodology_docs if ...]
                   if len(matches) == 0:
                       status = "MISSING"
                   elif not matches[0].content_markdown or not
  matches[0].content_markdown.strip():
                       status = "EMPTY"
                   else:
                       status = "OK"

   Objective

   Create tools/validate_schemas.py - CLI tool to validate Pass 2 step packages
  against JSON schemas (Gate B
   criterion: schema validation).

   Note: Per docs/DECISIONS.md, trace validation (tools/verify_traces.py and
  /workflows/{id}/traceability

   - Each package (ProblemDefinitionPackage, RequirementsPackage,
  ObjectivesPackage) validates against its
   JSON schema
   - Packages stored in content_jsonb field with artifact_type="step_package",
  pass_type="execution"

   File to Create

   | File                      | Action | Purpose
  |
   
  |---------------------------|--------|----------------------------------------
  -------|
   | tools/validate_schemas.py | Create | Self-contained CLI tool for schema
  validation |

   No modifications to production code in apps/api/.

   CLI Specification

   Arguments

   | Argument       | Type   | Required | Default       | Description
  |
   
  |----------------|--------|----------|---------------|------------------------
  --------------|
   | --workflow-id  | STRING | Yes      | —             | Public workflow ID
  (e.g., wf-abc123) |
   | --steps        | INT... | No       | 1 2 3         | Steps to validate
  |
   | --database-url | STRING | No       | From settings | Override DB connection
  |
   | --verbose / -v | Flag   | No       | False         | Show detailed
  validation errors      |

   Exit Codes

   | Code | Meaning                                                  |
   |------|----------------------------------------------------------|
   | 0    | PASS - All expected step packages found and schema-valid |
   | 1    | FAIL - Missing packages or schema validation errors      |
   | 2    | ERROR - Database connection failed                       |
   | 3    | ERROR - Workflow not found                               |

   Behavior

   1. Resolve workflow ID: Accept public workflow_id string → resolve to
  internal UUID via WorkflowRepository
   2. Query step packages for each step:
     - Use ArtifactRepository.get_step_package(workflow_id, step_name)
     - Filter: artifact_type = "step_package", pass_type = "execution"
   3. Validate each package against JSON schema:
     - Load schema via application/schemas.get_schema_for_step(step_name)
     - Use jsonschema.Draft7Validator to validate content_jsonb
   4. Report status per package:
     - VALID - Package exists and passes schema validation
     - INVALID - Package exists but fails schema validation (list errors)
     - MISSING - No package found for step
   5. Exit 0 only if all requested steps have VALID packages

   Output Format

   Gate B Schema Validation Report
   ===============================
   Workflow: wf-abc123
   Steps: 1, 2, 3

   Step 1: problem_definition
   --------------------------
   Package: ProblemDefinitionPackage
   Revision: 1
   Status: VALID

   Step 2: requirements
   --------------------------
   Package: RequirementsPackage
   Revision: 2
   Status: INVALID
   Errors:
     - path: ['requirements', 0, 'id']
       message: 'INVALID-ID' does not match '^(FR|NFR|CR|IR)-\\d{3}$'

   Step 3: objectives
   --------------------------
   Status: MISSING

   Summary
   =======
   Expected: 3
   Valid:    1
   Invalid:  1
   Missing:  1
   Status:   FAIL

   Implementation Approach

   Self-Contained Design (Following verify_methodology.py Pattern)

   #!/usr/bin/env python3
   import sys, os
   sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "apps",
  "api"))

   from infrastructure.db.models import StepName, PassType
   from infrastructure.db.repositories import ArtifactRepository,
  WorkflowRepository
   from application.schemas import get_schema_for_step
   import jsonschema

   Key Query Logic

   # Get step package (latest revision)
   artifact = await artifact_repo.get_step_package(
       workflow_id=workflow_uuid,
       step_name=step_name,  # StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION, etc.
       revision=None,  # Gets latest
   )

   if artifact is None:
       status = "MISSING"
   elif artifact.content_jsonb is None:
       status = "EMPTY"
   else:
       # Validate against schema
       schema = get_schema_for_step(step_name)
       validator = jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema)
       errors = list(validator.iter_errors(artifact.content_jsonb))
       status = "VALID" if not errors else "INVALID"

   Step-to-Schema Mapping

   | Step | StepName Enum      | Package Type             | Schema File
  |
   
  |------|--------------------|--------------------------|----------------------
  ----------|
   | 1    | PROBLEM_DEFINITION | ProblemDefinitionPackage |
  problem_definition.schema.json |
   | 2    | REQUIREMENTS       | RequirementsPackage      |
  requirements.schema.json       |
   | 3    | OBJECTIVES         | ObjectivesPackage        |
  objectives.schema.json         |

   # Help

   python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123

   # Verbose output with errors
   python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123 --verbose

   # Specific steps only
   python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123 --steps 1 2

│  50                                                                          │
│  51 class ValidationResult:                                                  │
│  52     def __init__(self):                                                  │
│  53         self.total_expected = 0                                          │
│  54         self.valid_count = 0                                             │
│  55         self.invalid_count = 0                                           │
│  56         self.missing_count = 0                                           │
│  57         self.empty_count = 0                                             │
│  58         self.details: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}                     │
│  59                                                                          │
│  60 async def get_workflow_uuid(session: AsyncSession, public_id: str) ->    │
│     Optional[UUID]:                                                          │
│  61     """Resolve public workflow_id string to internal UUID."""            │
│  62     stmt = select(Workflow).where(Workflow.workflow_id == public_id)     │
│  63     result = await session.execute(stmt)                                 │
│  64     workflow = result.scalar_one_or_none()                               │
│  65     return workflow.id if workflow else None                             │
│  66                                                                          │
│  67 async def validate_schemas(                                              │
│  68     workflow_id_str: str,                                                │
│  69     steps: List[int],                                                    │
│  70     db_url: Optional[str] = None,                                        │
│  71     verbose: bool = False                                                │
│  72 ) -> int:                                                                │
│  73     """Main validation logic."""                                         │
│  74     url = db_url or settings.postgres_url                                │
│  75                                                                          │
│  76     try:                                                                 │
│  77         engine = create_async_engine(url)                                │
│  78         async_session = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=     │
│     False)                                                                   │
│  79     except Exception as e:                                               │
│  80         print(f"Error connecting to database: {e}", file=sys.stderr)     │
│  81         return 2                                                         │
│  82                                                                          │
│  83     result = ValidationResult()                                          │
│  84                                                                          │
│  85     async with async_session() as session:                               │
│  86         # 1. Resolve workflow ID                                         │
│  87         workflow_uuid = await get_workflow_uuid(session,                 │
│     workflow_id_str)                                                         │
│  88         if not workflow_uuid:                                            │
│  89             print(f"Error: Workflow '{workflow_id_str}' not found.",     │
│     file=sys.stderr)                                                         │
│  90             await engine.dispose()                                       │
│  91             return 3                                                     │
│  92                                                                          │
│  93         repo = ArtifactRepository(session)                               │
│  94                                                                          │
│  95         # 2. Validate each step                                          │
│  96         for step_num in steps:                                           │
│  97             result.total_expected += 1                                   │
│  98             step_name = STEP_NUMBER_MAP.get(step_num)                    │
│  99                                                                          │
│ 100             if not step_name:                                            │
│ 101                 print(f"Warning: Step {step_num} not supported (only     │
│     1-3). Skipping.")                                                        │
│ 102                 continue                                                 │
│ 103                                                                          │
│ 104             artifact = await repo.get_step_package(                      │
│ 105                 workflow_id=workflow_uuid,                               │
│ 106                 step_name=step_name,                                     │
│ 107                 revision=None # Get latest                               │
│ 108             )                                                            │
│ 109                                                                          │
│ 110             step_detail = {                                              │
│ 111                 "step_name": step_name.value,                            │
│ 112                 "status": "MISSING",                                     │
│ 113                 "errors": []                                             │
│ 114             }                                                            │
│ 115                                                                          │
│ 116             if not artifact:                                             │
│ 117                 result.missing_count += 1                                │
│ 118             elif artifact.content_jsonb is None:                         │
│ 119                 step_detail["status"] = "EMPTY"                          │
│ 120                 result.empty_count += 1                                  │
│ 121             else:                                                        │
│ 122                 # Schema Validation                                      │
│ 123                 try:                                                     │
│ 124                     schema = get_schema_for_step(step_name)              │
│ 125                     validator = jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema)       │
│ 126                     errors = sorted                                      │
│     (validator.iter_errors(artifact.content_jsonb), key=lambda e: e.path)    │
│ 127                                                                          │
│ 128                     if not errors:                                       │
│ 129                         step_detail["status"] = "VALID"                  │
│ 130                         result.valid_count += 1                          │
│ 131                     else:                                                │
│ 132                         step_detail["status"] = "INVALID"                │
│ 133                         result.invalid_count += 1                        │
│ 134                         for error in errors:                             │
│ 135                             step_detail["errors"].append({               │
│ 136                                 "path": list(error.path),                │
│ 137                                 "message": error.message                 │
│ 138                             })                                           │
│ 139                 except Exception as e:                                   │
│ 140                     step_detail["status"] = "ERROR"                      │
│ 141                     step_detail["errors"].append({"message":             │
│     f"Validation exception: {str(e)}"})                                      │
│ 142                     result.invalid_count += 1                            │
│ 143                                                                          │
│ 144             result.details[step_num] = step_detail                       │
│ 145                                                                          │
│ 146     await engine.dispose()                                               │
│ 147                                                                          │
│ 148     # 3. Print Report                                                    │
│ 149     print_report(workflow_id_str, result, verbose)                       │
│ 150                                                                          │
│ 151     # 4. Exit Code                                                       │
│ 152     if result.valid_count == result.total_expected:                      │
│ 153         return 0                                                         │
│ 154     else:                                                                │
│ 155         return 1                                                         │
│ 156                                                                          │
│ 157 def print_report(workflow_id: str, result: ValidationResult, verbose:    │
│     bool):                                                                   │
│ 158     print("Gate B Schema Validation Report")                             │
│ 159     print("===============================")                             │
│ 160     print(f"Workflow: {workflow_id}")                                    │
│ 161     print(f"Steps: {', '.join(map(str, sorted(result.details.keys())))}  │
│     \n")                                                                     │
│ 162                                                                          │
│ 163     for step_num, detail in sorted(result.details.items()):              │
│ 164         print(f"Step {step_num}: {detail['step_name']}")                 │
│ 165         print("-" * (len(f"Step {step_num}: {detail['step_name']}")))    │
│ 166         print(f"Status: {detail['status']}")                             │
│ 167                                                                          │
│ 168         if detail['status'] == "INVALID":                                │
│ 169             print("Errors:")                                             │
│ 170             if verbose:                                                  │
│ 171                 for err in detail['errors']:                             │
│ 172                     path_str = " -> ".join(map(str, err.get('path',      │
│     []))) or "(root)"                                                        │
│ 173                     print(f"  - [{path_str}]: {err['message']}")         │
│ 174             else:                                                        │
│ 175                 print(f"  - {len(detail['errors'])} schema violations    │
│     found. Use --verbose to see details.")                                   │
│ 176         elif detail['status'] == "ERROR":                                │
│ 177              print(f"Error: {detail['errors'][0]['message']}")           │
│ 178                                                                          │
│ 179         print()                                                          │
│ 180                                                                          │
│ 181     print("Summary")                                                     │
│ 182     print("=======")                                                     │
│ 183     print(f"Expected: {result.total_expected}")                          │
│ 184     print(f"Valid:    {result.valid_count}")                             │
│ 185     print(f"Invalid:  {result.invalid_count}")                           │
│ 186     print(f"Missing:  {result.missing_count}")                           │
│ 187     if result.empty_count > 0:                                           │
│ 188         print(f"Empty:    {result.empty_count}")                         │
│ 189                                                                          │
│ 190     print(f"Status:   {'PASS' if result.valid_count ==                   │
│     result.total_expected else 'FAIL'}")                                     │
│ 191                                                                          │

  Senior Developer Plan: P6.2 - Gate B Schema Validation Tool

  1. Objective
  Create tools/validate_schemas.py, a CLI tool to validate Pass 2 artifact
  packages (Steps 1-3) against the project's authoritative JSON schemas
  (packages/contracts/*.schema.json). This satisfies the schema validation
  portion of Acceptance Gate B.

  2. Directives (Strict Adherence Required)
   * Traceability Deferred: Do NOT implement verify_traces.py or any trace
     verification logic. Focus ONLY on schema validation as per
     docs/DECISIONS.md.
   * No Production Changes: Do NOT modify any code in apps/api/. Use existing
     repositories and models as read-only.
   * Self-Contained: The tool must reside entirely within
     tools/validate_schemas.py.
   * Use Existing Schemas: Load schemas using
     apps.api.application.schemas.get_schema_for_step to ensure single source of
     truth.
   * Step Scope: Only support Steps 1, 2, and 3 (MVP scope).
   * Exit Codes: Strict adherence to 0 (Pass), 1 (Fail), 2 (DB Error), 3 (No
     Workflow).

  3. Negative Patterns to Avoid
   * DO NOT modify apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py to add
     convenience methods.
   * DO NOT default to "all workflows" or "latest workflow" implicitly; require
     --workflow-id string to be explicit.
   * DO NOT suppress jsonschema validation errors; report them clearly (verbose
     mode) or summarily (default).
   * DO NOT use sys.path.append; use sys.path.insert(0, ...) to prioritize the
     local apps/api module.
   * DO NOT rely on relative file paths for schemas within the tool; delegate to
     the existing application.schemas loader.

  4. Implementation Specification

   * File: projects/solver-engine/tools/validate_schemas.py
   * Imports: asyncio, argparse, sys, os, json, uuid, jsonschema.
   * Dependencies (Imported from App):
       * config.settings
       * infrastructure.db.models.Workflow
       * infrastructure.db.repositories.ArtifactRepository
       * domain.state.StepName
       * application.schemas.get_schema_for_step

   * Logic Flow:
       1. Parse args (--workflow-id, --steps, --verbose).
       2. Connect to DB (async).
       3. Resolve workflow_id string -> UUID. Exit 3 if not found.
       4. Iterate steps [1, 2, 3]:
          a. Get step package via ArtifactRepository.get_step_package.
          b. If missing -> Status MISSING.
          c. If present ->
  jsonschema.Draft7Validator(get_schema_for_step(step_name)).iter_errors(content
  ).
          d. If errors -> Status INVALID (collect errors).
          e. Else -> Status VALID.
       5. Print Report.
       6. Exit 0 if all VALID, else 1.

  5. Verification Command
  After implementation, run:
   1 python tools/validate_schemas.py --help
  (Should print usage without import errors).

  6. Execution
  Proceed with creating tools/validate_schemas.py.

> For information only. <information only> ⏺ Evaluation of Co-Developer-1
  Feedback

    Co-Developer-1 raises several excellent points. Let me analyze each:

    1. Query Strategy: get_step_package() vs list_for_workflow() + filtering

    Co-Developer-1's approach:
    artifacts = await artifact_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_uuid)
    step_packages = [a for a in artifacts
        if a.artifact_type == "step_package"
        and a.pass_type == PassType.EXECUTION]

    My plan's approach:
    artifact = await artifact_repo.get_step_package(workflow_id, step_name)

    My perspective: For a verification tool, Co-Developer-1's explicit filtering
  approach is superior. Verification tools should be transparent about what
  they're checking - relying on repository method internals is less auditable.
  I'll adopt this approach.

    2. StepName Enum Confusion

    Co-Developer-1 warns: "Don't mix domain.state.StepName and
  infrastructure.db.models.StepName"

    My perspective: This is a critical catch! Looking at the code:
    - application/schemas.py imports from domain.state import StepName
    - infrastructure/db/models/ has its own StepName enum
    - These may be the same enum or may differ

    Resolution: Use explicit step_number → domain.state.StepName mapping for
  schema lookup:
    from domain.state import StepName as DomainStepName

    STEP_NUMBER_TO_DOMAIN_STEP = {
        1: DomainStepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION,
        2: DomainStepName.REQUIREMENTS,
        3: DomainStepName.OBJECTIVES,
    }

    3. Status Values: EMPTY

    Co-Developer-1 adds: EMPTY status for content_jsonb = None

    My perspective: Agreed. A package could exist with null content - this is
  distinct from MISSING (no artifact) and INVALID (failed validation). Adding
  EMPTY maintains parity with verify_methodology.py.

    4. Package Type Mismatch

    Co-Developer-1 suggests: "optionally flag package_type mismatch"

    Question: Should mismatch be FAIL or warning?

    My perspective: Treat as FAIL (INVALID). If step 2 has package_type =
  "ProblemDefinitionPackage" instead of "RequirementsPackage", that's a
  structural error indicating data corruption or bug - not a valid state.

    5. Co-Developer-1's Questions

    | Question                                 | My Recommendation
  |
    
  |------------------------------------------|----------------------------------
  ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  --------------------------|
    | --workflow-id required vs --latest flag? | Keep required. Verification
  tools should target explicit workflows. A --latest flag adds complexity
  without clear benefit for Gate B.    |
    | Package type mismatch: FAIL or warning?  | FAIL (INVALID). Structural
  integrity matters.
  |
    | Latest revision only or all revisions?   | Latest only (default). Gate B
  asks "does valid package exist?" not "are all revisions valid?" Could add
  --all-revisions later if needed. |

    ---
    Updated Plan Recommendations

    I'll update the plan to incorporate these refinements:

    1. Use explicit filtering (list_for_workflow() + filter) instead of
  get_step_package()
    2. Use domain.state.StepName explicitly for schema lookup with step_number
  mapping
    3. Add EMPTY status for null content_jsonb
    4. Validate package_type matches expected value (mismatch = INVALID)
    5. Keep --workflow-id required
    6. Validate latest revision only (per step)

       P6.2 Plan: Gate B Schema Validation Tool (Revised)

       Objective

       Create tools/validate_schemas.py - CLI tool to validate Pass 2 step
  packages against JSON schemas 
       (Gate B criterion: schema validation).

       Note: Per docs/DECISIONS.md, trace validation (tools/verify_traces.py and
       /workflows/{id}/traceability endpoint) is deferred. This slice focuses
  ONLY on schema validation.

       Gate B Criterion (Schema Portion)

       - Step 1-3 Pass 2 packages exist with valid schemas
       - Each package (ProblemDefinitionPackage, RequirementsPackage,
  ObjectivesPackage) validates against
        its JSON schema
       - Packages stored in content_jsonb field with
  artifact_type="step_package", pass_type="execution"

       File to Create

       | File                      | Action | Purpose
  |
       
  |---------------------------|--------|----------------------------------------
  -------|
       | tools/validate_schemas.py | Create | Self-contained CLI tool for schema
  validation |

       No modifications to production code in apps/api/.

       CLI Specification

       Arguments

       | Argument       | Type   | Required | Default       | Description
  |
       
  |----------------|--------|----------|---------------|------------------------
  --------------|
       | --workflow-id  | STRING | Yes      | —             | Public workflow ID
  (e.g., wf-abc123) |
       | --steps        | INT... | No       | 1 2 3         | Steps to validate
  |
       | --database-url | STRING | No       | From settings | Override DB
  connection               |
       | --verbose / -v | Flag   | No       | False         | Show detailed
  validation errors      |

       Exit Codes

       | Code | Meaning                                                  |
       |------|----------------------------------------------------------|
       | 0    | PASS - All expected step packages found and schema-valid |
       | 1    | FAIL - Missing, empty, or invalid packages               |
       | 2    | ERROR - Database connection failed                       |
       | 3    | ERROR - Workflow not found                               |

       Behavior

       1. Resolve workflow ID: Accept public workflow_id string → resolve to
  internal UUID via 
       WorkflowRepository
       2. Query step packages with explicit filtering:
         - Use ArtifactRepository.list_for_workflow(workflow_id)
         - Filter explicitly: artifact_type == "step_package" AND pass_type ==
  PassType.EXECUTION
         - Group by step_number, select latest revision per step
       3. Validate each package against JSON schema:
         - Map step_number → domain.state.StepName (NOT
  infrastructure.db.models.StepName)
         - Load schema via
  application/schemas.get_schema_for_step(domain_step_name)
         - Validate package_type matches expected value (mismatch = INVALID)
         - Use jsonschema.Draft7Validator to validate content_jsonb
       4. Report status per package:
         - VALID - Package exists, correct type, passes schema validation
         - INVALID - Package exists but fails validation or wrong package_type
         - EMPTY - Package exists but content_jsonb is None
         - MISSING - No package found for step
       5. Exit 0 only if all requested steps have VALID packages

       Output Format

       Gate B Schema Validation Report
       ===============================
       Workflow: wf-abc123
       Steps: 1, 2, 3

       Step 1: problem_definition
       --------------------------
       Package: ProblemDefinitionPackage
       Revision: 1
       Status: VALID

       Step 2: requirements
       --------------------------
       Package: RequirementsPackage
       Revision: 2
       Status: INVALID
       Errors:
         - path: ['requirements', 0, 'id']
           message: 'INVALID-ID' does not match '^(FR|NFR|CR|IR)-\\d{3}$'

       Step 3: objectives
       --------------------------
       Status: MISSING

       Summary
       =======
       Expected: 3
       Valid:    1
       Invalid:  1
       Missing:  1
       Status:   FAIL

       Implementation Approach

       Self-Contained Design (Following verify_methodology.py Pattern)

       #!/usr/bin/env python3
       import sys, os
       sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "apps",
  "api"))

       # Use domain.state.StepName for schema lookup (NOT
  infrastructure.db.models.StepName)
       from domain.state import StepName as DomainStepName
       from infrastructure.db.models import PassType
       from infrastructure.db.repositories import ArtifactRepository,
  WorkflowRepository
       from application.schemas import get_schema_for_step
       import jsonschema

       Step Number Mappings (Critical: Use domain.state.StepName)

       # Map step_number → domain.state.StepName for schema lookup
       STEP_NUMBER_TO_DOMAIN_STEP: Dict[int, DomainStepName] = {
           1: DomainStepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION,
           2: DomainStepName.REQUIREMENTS,
           3: DomainStepName.OBJECTIVES,
       }

       # Expected package_type per step (for structural validation)
       EXPECTED_PACKAGE_TYPE: Dict[int, str] = {
           1: "ProblemDefinitionPackage",
           2: "RequirementsPackage",
           3: "ObjectivesPackage",
       }

       Key Query Logic (Explicit Filtering)

       # Fetch all artifacts and filter explicitly (transparent for verification
  tool)
       all_artifacts = await artifact_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_uuid)

       # Filter to Pass 2 step packages only
       step_packages = [
           a for a in all_artifacts
           if a.artifact_type == "step_package"
           and a.pass_type == PassType.EXECUTION
           and a.step_number in steps
       ]

       # Group by step_number, get latest revision per step
       for step_num in steps:
           matches = [a for a in step_packages if a.step_number == step_num]

           if not matches:
               status = "MISSING"
           else:
               # Get latest revision
               artifact = max(matches, key=lambda a: a.revision)

               if artifact.content_jsonb is None:
                   status = "EMPTY"
               elif artifact.package_type != EXPECTED_PACKAGE_TYPE[step_num]:
                   status = "INVALID"  # Wrong package type
                   errors = [{"message": f"Expected
  {EXPECTED_PACKAGE_TYPE[step_num]}, got 
       {artifact.package_type}"}]
               else:
                   # Validate against schema using domain.state.StepName
                   domain_step = STEP_NUMBER_TO_DOMAIN_STEP[step_num]
                   schema = get_schema_for_step(domain_step)
                   validator = jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema)
                   errors = list(validator.iter_errors(artifact.content_jsonb))
                   status = "VALID" if not errors else "INVALID"

       Step-to-Schema Mapping

       | Step | StepName Enum      | Package Type             | Schema File
  |
       
  |------|--------------------|--------------------------|----------------------
  ----------|
       | 1    | PROBLEM_DEFINITION | ProblemDefinitionPackage |
  problem_definition.schema.json |
       | 2    | REQUIREMENTS       | RequirementsPackage      |
  requirements.schema.json       |
       | 3    | OBJECTIVES         | ObjectivesPackage        |
  objectives.schema.json         |

       Verification Commands

       # Help
       python tools/validate_schemas.py --help

       # Validate all steps for workflow
       python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123

       # Verbose output with errors
       python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123 --verbose

       # Specific steps only
       python tools/validate_schemas.py --workflow-id wf-abc123 --steps 1 2

       Dependencies (Read-Only)

       - apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py -
  get_step_package()
       - apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/workflow.py -
  get_by_workflow_id()
       - apps/api/infrastructure/db/models/ - StepName, PassType
       - apps/api/application/schemas.py - get_schema_for_step()
       - packages/contracts/*.schema.json - Step 1-3 JSON schemas
       - apps/api/config.py - settings.postgres_url

       Not In Scope (Deferred per docs/DECISIONS.md)

       - tools/verify_traces.py - Traceability link verification
       - /workflows/{id}/traceability endpoint
       - Trace link validation (coverage_map, trace_map checks)

       Negative Patterns to Avoid (per Co-Developer-1)

       1. Don't mix StepName enums: Use domain.state.StepName for schema lookup,
  NOT 
       infrastructure.db.models.StepName
       2. Don't skip explicit filtering: Use list_for_workflow() + explicit
  artifact_type/pass_type 
       filters for transparency
       3. Don't implement deferred features: No traceability endpoint or
  verify_traces.py
       4. Don't default to "all workflows": Require explicit --workflow-id
       5. Don't bypass get_schema_for_step(): Use the application layer's schema
  loader, not hard-coded 
       paths

       Design Decisions

       | Decision                        | Rationale                            
               |
       
  |---------------------------------|-------------------------------------------
  ---------------------
       --------|
       | --workflow-id required          | Verification tools should target
  explicit workflows            
               |
       | Package type mismatch = INVALID | Structural integrity matters; wrong
  type indicates data 
       corruption     |
       | Latest revision only            | Gate B asks "does valid package
  exist?" not "are all revisions 
       valid?" |
       | Explicit filtering              | Verification tool should be
  transparent about what it checks   
               |
       | EMPTY status added              | Distinct from MISSING (no artifact)
  and INVALID (failed 
       validation)    |

       Test Scenarios

       1. --help shows usage
       2. Non-existent workflow returns exit code 3
       3. Workflow with valid packages returns exit code 0
       4. Workflow with missing packages returns exit code 1
       5. Workflow with invalid packages returns exit code 1 + errors in verbose
  mode
       6. Workflow with empty content_jsonb returns exit code 1 (EMPTY status)
       7. Workflow with wrong package_type returns exit code 1 (INVALID status) 
  </information only> no action required.

✦ Here is the analysis and plan for Slice P6.3: Gate C Verification (Gate
  Enforcement).

  Analysis
   * Goal: Implement the verification logic for Gate C: Gating Enforced.
   * Requirement: Verify three critical invariants (Doc 3 Appendix A):
       1. Mandatory Approval: Pass 2 cannot advance from awaiting_review without
          an explicit approve action.
       2. Message Safety: Sending a message while awaiting_review records the
          message but does not change state or advance the workflow.
       3. Revision Loop: Sending revise transitions to revision_requested and
          eventually loops back to awaiting_review (after re-execution).
   * Target: make test-gates.
   * Context: This requires executing the workflow state machine. Since the
     actual LLM generation is slow/costly, we should use a mock LLM adapter or a
     test-specific configuration to speed up the "work" phase so we can focus on
     the gate transitions.
   * Existing Infrastructure:
       * apps/api/orchestration/graph.py (The State Graph).
       * apps/api/orchestration/nodes.py (The Nodes).
       * tests/ directory structure.

  Plan (P6.3)

  1. Create Test File: `tests/integration/test_gate_enforcement.py`
   * Type: pytest async test.
   * Scope: Integration test using the actual LangGraph definition
     (create_graph) and SolverCheckpointSaver.
   * Setup:
       * Initialize a workflow in memory (or using a test DB session).
       * Mock the "generation" nodes (analyze, structure) to return immediate
         dummy output so the graph quickly reaches awaiting_review.
   * Test Cases:
       * test_cannot_advance_without_approval():
           * Run graph -> reaches awaiting_review.
           * Assert status is AWAITING_REVIEW.
           * Try to resume with None or invalid action -> Assert status remains
             AWAITING_REVIEW or raises error.
       * test_message_does_not_advance():
           * Run graph -> reaches awaiting_review.
           * Send message action.
           * Assert status remains AWAITING_REVIEW.
           * Assert message count increased.
       * test_revision_loop():
           * Run graph -> reaches awaiting_review.
           * Send revise action (Reject).
           * Assert status becomes REVISION_REQUESTED.
           * Resume graph (simulating agent rework).
           * Assert status returns to AWAITING_REVIEW.
       * test_approval_advances():
           * Run graph -> reaches awaiting_review.
           * Send approve action.
           * Assert status becomes APPROVED / COMPLETE.
           * Assert graph moves to next step (or end).

  2. Update `Makefile`
   * Add target test-gates:
   1     test-gates:
   2         pytest tests/integration/test_gate_enforcement.py -v

  3. Execution Strategy
   * This relies on pytest-asyncio.
   * We need to ensure the test environment can instantiate the graph without
     full infrastructure (e.g., real OpenAI API). We will patch the LLM calls.

  Proposed Verification
   * Immediate: Run make test-gates.
   * Success: All tests pass, confirming the state machine enforces strict
     gating.

     Add to apps/api/tests/integration/test_orchestration_interrupt_resume.py:

     @pytest.mark.asyncio
     async def test_reject_revise_loops_back_to_review(self, checkpointer):
         """REJECT action triggers re-execution and returns to AWAITING_REVIEW.

         Gate C Scenario C3:
         GIVEN a workflow in awaiting_review status
         WHEN I apply REJECT (revise) with feedback
         THEN:
           - Workflow re-executes step with feedback context
           - Eventually returns to awaiting_review status
           - Step does NOT advance (still on same step)
         """
         graph = create_graph(checkpointer)
         thread_id = make_thread_id()
         checkpointer._test_thread_ids.append(thread_id)
         config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

         initial_state = make_initial_state(thread_id, PassType.EXECUTION)

         # Run to interrupt at review
         result1 = await graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)
         assert_interrupted(result1)
         assert result1["step_state"].status == StepStatus.AWAITING_REVIEW
         original_step = result1["current_step"]

         # Apply REJECT with feedback
         await graph.aupdate_state(
             config,
             {
                 "human_decision": HumanAction.REJECT,
                 "human_feedback": "Please add more detail to the analysis",
             },
             as_node="review",
         )

         # Resume - should re-execute and return to review
         result2 = await graph.ainvoke(None, config)

         # Should interrupt again at same step (re-execution complete)
         assert_interrupted(result2)
         assert result2["current_step"] == original_step  # Same step
         assert result2["step_state"].status == StepStatus.AWAITING_REVIEW

    2     async def test_reject_revise_loops_back_to_review(self, checkpointer):
    3         """REJECT action triggers re-execution and returns to 
      AWAITING_REVIEW.
    4 
    5         Gate C Scenario C3:
    6         GIVEN a workflow in awaiting_review status
    7         WHEN I apply REJECT (revise) with feedback
    8         THEN:
    9           - Workflow re-executes step with feedback context
   10           - Eventually returns to awaiting_review status
   11           - Step does NOT advance (still on same step)
   12         """
   13         graph = create_graph(checkpointer)
   14         thread_id = make_thread_id()
   15         checkpointer._test_thread_ids.append(thread_id)
   16         config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
   17 
   18         initial_state = make_initial_state(thread_id, PassType.EXECUTION)
   19 
   20         # Run to interrupt at review
   21         result1 = await graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)
   22         assert_interrupted(result1)
   23         
   24         # Verify initial state
   25         step_state = result1.get("step_state")
   26         status = step_state.status if hasattr(step_state, "status") else 
      step_state.get("status")
   27         assert status == StepStatus.AWAITING_REVIEW
   28         
   29         original_step = result1["current_step"]
   30 
   31         # Apply REJECT with feedback
   32         await graph.aupdate_state(
   33             config,
   34             {
   35                 "human_decision": HumanAction.REJECT,
   36                 "human_feedback": "Please add more detail to the analysis"
   37             },
   38             as_node="review",
   39         )
   40 
   41         # Resume - should re-execute and return to review
   42         result2 = await graph.ainvoke(None, config)
   43 
   44         # Should interrupt again at same step (re-execution complete)
   45         assert_interrupted(result2)
   46         assert result2["current_step"] == original_step  # Same step
   47         
   48         step_state2 = result2.get("step_state")
   49         status2 = step_state2.status if hasattr(step_state2, "status") els
      step_state2.get("status")
   50         assert status2 == StepStatus.AWAITING_REVIEW

      stubbing generate_step_output/validate_output, which will hit

      orchestration.nodes.generate_step_output and

      schema complexities.

      validation.

    - Skipping Makefile help/.PHONY updates for new targets.

│ async def review_node(state: WorkflowState) -> WorkflowState:                │
│     """Raise interrupt for human review.                                     │
│                                                                              │
│     Gate policy enforcement:                                                 │
│     - Pass 1 + NONE: auto-approve                                            │
│     - Pass 1 + END_OF_PASS + step < 10: auto-approve                         │
│     - Pass 2: always requires human approval                                 │
│     """                                                                      │
│     state = coerce_state(state)                                              │
│     # Gate policy enforcement (Pass 1 only)                                  │
│     if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:                            │
│         if state.gate_policy == GatePolicy.NONE:                             │
│             state.human_decision = HumanAction.APPROVE                       │
│             return state                                                     │
│         elif state.gate_policy == GatePolicy.END_OF_PASS:                    │
│             step_num = STEP_NUMBERS[state.current_step]                      │
│             if step_num < 10:                                                │
│                 state.human_decision = HumanAction.APPROVE                   │
│                 return state                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│     # Pass 2 always requires human approval, or Pass 1 with PER_STEP policy  │
│     interrupt({                                                              │
│         "gate_type": "review",                                               │
│         "output": state.step_state.output,                                   │
│         "validation": state.step_state.validation_result.__dict__ if         │
│ state.step_state.validation_result else None,                                │
│         "step_name": state.current_step.value,                               │
│         "pass_type": state.current_pass.value,                               │
│     })                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│     return state                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ async def process_decision_node(state: WorkflowState) -> WorkflowState:      │
│     """Process human decision.                                               │
│                                                                              │
│     Updates state based on human action. Does NOT clear human_decision       │
│     (routing depends on it; cleared in target nodes per DECISIONS.md P3.3).  │
│     """                                                                      │
│     state = coerce_state(state)                                              │
│     if state.human_decision == HumanAction.APPROVE:                          │
│         state.step_state.status = StepStatus.APPROVED                        │
│         state.step_state.phase = StepPhase.COMPLETE                          │
│         state.step_state.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()                    │
│                                                                              │
│     elif state.human_decision == HumanAction.REJECT:                         │
│         state.step_state.status = StepStatus.REVISION_REQUESTED              │
│         state.step_state.human_feedback = state.human_feedback               │
│         state.step_state.phase = StepPhase.STRUCTURING                       │
│                                                                              │
│     elif state.human_decision == HumanAction.MODIFY:                         │
│         # Guard against None output                                          │
│         if state.step_state.output is not None and                           │
│ state.human_modifications:                                                   │
│             state.step_state.output.update(state.human_modifications)        │
│         state.step_state.phase = StepPhase.VALIDATING                        │
│                                                                              │
│     state.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()                                     │
│     return state                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ async def advance_node(state: WorkflowState) -> WorkflowState:               │
│     """Advance to next step.                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│     Clears human_decision (approve path), stores artifact,                   │
│     and transitions to next step.                                            │
│     """                                                                      │
│     state = coerce_state(state)                                              │
│     # Clear human_decision (approve path cleanup)                            │
│     _clear_human_decision(state)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│     # Store artifact                                                         │
│     step_key = state.current_step.value                                      │
│     if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:                            │
│         state.methodology[step_key] = state.step_state.output                │
│     else:                                                                    │
│         state.artifacts[step_key] = state.step_state.output                  │
│                                                                              │
│     # Determine next position (MVP: Steps 1-3 only)                          │
│     current_num = STEP_NUMBERS[state.current_step]                           │
│                                                                              │
│     if current_num < 3:                                                      │
│         # Advance to next step within same pass                              │
│         next_step = [k for k, v in STEP_NUMBERS.items() if v == current_num  │
│ + 1][0]                                                                      │
│         state.current_step = next_step                                       │
│     elif state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:                          │
│         # End of Pass 1 Step 3 → Pass 2 Step 1                               │
│         state.current_pass = PassType.EXECUTION                              │
│         state.current_step = StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION                     │
│     # else: workflow complete (handled by graph edge)                        │
│                                                                              │
│     # Reset step state for new step                                          │
│     state.step_state = StepState(                                            │
│         step_name=state.current_step,                                        │
│         step_number=STEP_NUMBERS[state.current_step],                        │
│         pass_type=state.current_pass,                                        │
│     )                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│     state.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()                                     │
│     return state                                                             │

   """
   Gate D Integration Tests - Restart/Recovery

   P6.4: Tests Gate D criterion:
     "Restart + resume works mid-step and at awaiting_review with no lost
  artifacts."

   Uses monkeypatched LLM calls for deterministic, offline testing.

   class TestGateDRestartRecovery:
       """Gate D: State survives restart; resume works correctly."""

       @pytest.mark.asyncio
       async def test_state_survives_restart(self, stub_llm_nodes):
           """D1: State persists across process restart (engine
  dispose/recreate)."""

       @pytest.mark.asyncio
       async def test_resume_at_awaiting_review(self, stub_llm_nodes):
           """D2: Resume works when workflow interrupted at awaiting_review."""

           """D3: Can approve and advance workflow after restart."""

           """D4: Methodology dict/artifacts not lost on restart."""

   2. Restart Simulation Pattern

   # Phase 1: Run to interrupt with first checkpointer
   engine1 = create_async_engine(settings.postgres_url, echo=False)
   factory1 = async_sessionmaker(engine1, class_=AsyncSession,
  expire_on_commit=False)
   saver1 = SolverCheckpointSaver(factory1, engine=engine1)
   graph1 = create_graph(saver1)

   result1 = await graph1.ainvoke(initial_state, config)
   assert_interrupted(result1)

   # Dispose to simulate process death
   await engine1.dispose()

   # Phase 2: Create completely NEW everything, same thread_id
   engine2 = create_async_engine(settings.postgres_url, echo=False)
   factory2 = async_sessionmaker(engine2, class_=AsyncSession,
  expire_on_commit=False)
   saver2 = SolverCheckpointSaver(factory2, engine=engine2)
   graph2 = create_graph(saver2)

   # Verify state persisted
   snapshot = await graph2.aget_state(config)
   assert snapshot is not None, "State should persist after restart"
